2026-09-13 07:33:20 [info     ] stt.backend                    mode=real_ml size=tiny

── Backend detection ─────────────────────────────────────────
  Authenticity : is_real_ml=True  mode=real_ml
  Identity     : is_real_ml=True  mode=real_ml
  STT          : is_real_ml=True   mode=real_ml

── analyze_window type ───────────────────────────────────────
  Signature : (state: 'SessionState', pcm_bytes: 'bytes', pipeline_mode: 'str' = 'mock', stt_submit: "Optional[Callable[[int, 'np.ndarray'], None]]" = None) -> 'List[dict]'
  Is async  : False  ← MUST be False (synchronous)

── Execution path in _process() ──────────────────────────────
  if use_pool:
    await loop.run_in_executor(_ml_pool, lambda: analyze_window(...))
    └─ AASIST forward pass  } in pool thread — GIL released during torch
    └─ ECAPA forward pass   } in pool thread — GIL released during torch
  else (mock):
    analyze_window(...)  ← inline, instant
  await run_in_executor yields control → event loop free during inference

── STT submission (thread-safe) ──────────────────────────────
  _submit() uses loop.call_soon_threadsafe(stt_queue.submit, job)
  asyncio.Queue.put_nowait is NEVER called from a pool thread

── processing_lock behaviour ─────────────────────────────────
  async with state.processing_lock  (per-session asyncio.Lock)
  → same session: serialised   → different sessions: concurrent

── ⚠  BACKPRESSURE FINDING ───────────────────────────────────
  asyncio.create_task(_process(...))  has NO cap.
  Tasks accumulate waiting for processing_lock if arrival > inference rate.
  At real call cadence (1 chunk/s) + AASIST ~150 ms: backlog stays ≈ 0.
  At stress cadence (>6 chunks/s): tasks accumulate — UNBOUNDED.
  This is a KNOWN LIMITATION of Phase 1.6 design.
  Mitigation (semaphore/token-bucket) is OUT OF SCOPE for Phase 1.6.
